1495 — oblique crunch v. 2

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1495 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

An oblique crunch where you curl up and to the side, version 2. It trains the side abs.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. Place your hands behind your head or cross them over your chest.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1495.json#/instructions/0, 1495.json#/instructions/1

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1495.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. 1495.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place your hands behind your head or cross them over your chest. 1495.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your abs and lift your shoulder blades off the ground, rotating your torso to one side. 1495.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment, then lower your shoulder blades back down to the starting position. 1495.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat on the other side. 1495.json#/instructions/4
  6. Continue alternating sides for the desired number of repetitions. 1495.json#/instructions/5

1496 — sledge hammer

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1496 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Swinging a sledgehammer down onto a tire in a controlled arc. It trains the abs and obliques and also the shoulders and grip.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the sledge hammer with both hands.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1496.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the sledge hammer with both hands.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1496.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the sledge hammer with both hands. 1496.json#/instructions/0
  2. Engage your core and keep your back straight. 1496.json#/instructions/1
  3. Swing the sledge hammer down towards the ground, using your core and upper body strength. 1496.json#/instructions/2
  4. As you swing down, pivot your hips and transfer the force to the hammer. 1496.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 1496.json#/instructions/4

1497 — barbell reverse band bench press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1497 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supported_lying.

A bench press with bands that lighten the load at the bottom and add heavier tension near the top. It builds pressing strength.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Set up on a bench with bands anchored to the rack and attached to the bar. Lie back with your eyes under the bar and your shoulder blades pinched.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1497.json#/instructions/0, 1497.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set up on a bench with bands anchored to the rack and attached to the bar. 1497.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lie back with your eyes under the bar and your shoulder blades pinched. 1497.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower the bar to your mid chest with control. 1497.json#/instructions/2
  4. Press the bar back up to straight arms, feeling the band resistance change. 1497.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your feet planted and your wrists stacked over your elbows. 1497.json#/instructions/4

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1498 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supported_lying.

A decline sit-up with a band adding resistance. It trains the abs through a long range.

Proposed staging

1499 — band behind neck shoulder press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1499 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A band-resisted overhead press from behind the neck. It trains the shoulders and triceps with rising tension.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand on the band and hold the ends at the back of your shoulders. Brace your core and keep your head forward.

  2. Brace your core and keep your head forward. 1499.json#/instructions/1
  3. Press your hands straight up until your arms are extended. 1499.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower slowly back behind your head to shoulder level. 1499.json#/instructions/3
  5. Only use this variation if your shoulders move comfortably in that position. 1499.json#/instructions/4

1501 — band side plank row with partner

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1501 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: side_plank.

A side plank with a rowing motion against a band held by a partner. It trains the lats and trunk stability.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Get into a side plank on one forearm, with a partner holding the band in front of you. Grip the band with your top hand and keep your hips lifted.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1501.json#/instructions/0, 1501.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Grip the band with your top hand and keep your hips lifted.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1501.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Stack the feet comfortably on the documented support or preserve the source's staggered-foot/raised-leg variation; do not substitute a two-arm front plank.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 1501.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Resistance band base family. For this floor-support pose, stage the documented hand, forearm, foot or knee supports on a level ground plane unless the source names another surface; this is an authored support choice. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1501.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Get into a side plank on one forearm, with a partner holding the band in front of you. 1501.json#/instructions/0
  2. Grip the band with your top hand and keep your hips lifted. 1501.json#/instructions/1
  3. Row your hand back toward your ribs, squeezing the shoulder blade. 1501.json#/instructions/2
  4. Return slowly without letting your hips drop. 1501.json#/instructions/3
  5. Finish your reps, then switch sides. 1501.json#/instructions/4
